================================================================================================
BOOKING NOTES V5.3 -- 1000-PRODUCT AUDIT
================================================================================================

Prompt : SYSTEM_PROMPT_FH_BOOKING_V5_4 (25 columns, heading-gated)
         UNCHANGED from the 100-product run, deliberately -- the sample
         changed, so the prompt did not, or no difference could be
         attributed to either.
Model  : gpt-5.6-luna
Set    : 1000 products, UNIFORM RANDOM (seed 42), none of them from the
         600 already run. NOT stratified and NOT hardest-first -- every
         earlier booking set was chosen for difficulty, so this is the
         first booking run whose rate can be quoted for the catalogue.

A column fills ONLY when the supplier wrote a heading naming it. An EMPTY
column is a CORRECT answer. Text that names no column goes to `notes` --
that is the safety net, not a failure.

*** NOBODY HAS HAND-READ THESE 500. ***
Everything below is DETECTOR OUTPUT and is an UPPER BOUND. On the
100-product run, once each finding was read against the raw, 24 of 43
flagged products turned out to be the detector rather than the model.
The VERDICT and COMMENT lines are blank because this file is the INPUT
to a hand review, not the result of one.

One scorer bug was found and fixed DURING this run: the gate leak count
was 93 across 36 products before the fix and 49 across 18 after. The gate
split on markdown headings while the column mapping already honoured
inline labels, so products correctly routing off `Refund Policy - ...`
were accused of filling from nothing. On the 100 run that flag read 0
both before and after -- it was invisible until the sample changed.

products with something to look at : 177 of 1000
products with nothing flagged      : 823 of 1000

This file contains the FLAGGED products only. All 500 are in
exports/booking_v5_3_500_scores.xlsx, where they can be sorted and
filtered.

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FINDING COUNTS  (upper bounds -- unverified)
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
     16  ITINERARY LINE TEST -- no time, step or stop
      9  MARKDOWN JUNK -- * or # survived into a column
      5  URL LOST -- a link in the raw reached no column
      4  URL ALTERED -- a link was changed or invented
      4  GATE LEAK -- column filled and the raw offers NO licence
      3  ITEM AS HEADING -- a packing-list item opened its own column
      1  MID-SENTENCE START -- a value begins part-way through a sentence

    213  RECOVERED CONTENT -- raw text that reached no column
     18  REWORDED -- present but not verbatim (a VERBATIM defect)
     13  DUPLICATED -- same sentence in 2+ columns (reported, not removed)

------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
COLLAPSE -- how often one heading swallowed a whole product
------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

The measurement this run existed to produce. Reported BY STRATUM because
the overall figure is meaningless: 125 of the 500 were selected for
having no headings at all, and those collapse into the catch-all
CORRECTLY.

  heading_rich          263 of  702 at 100% in one column  (37.5%)  <-- THE REAL MEASURE
  long_no_heading        74 of   75 at 100% in one column  (98.7%)  (correct by design -- no headings exist)
  inline_label_only       5 of   10 at 100% in one column  (50.0%)  (correct by design -- no headings exist)

  21 products have THREE OR MORE headings and still filled exactly
  one column. Those are the outer-heading rule's real cost:
        345076    5 headings,   610 words  -> ALL in important_info
        488613    3 headings,   561 words  -> ALL in notes
        287265    3 headings,   433 words  -> ALL in notes
        686853    3 headings,   433 words  -> ALL in notes
        718818    5 headings,   284 words  -> ALL in disclaimers
        718817    5 headings,   278 words  -> ALL in disclaimers
        488611    3 headings,   224 words  -> ALL in notes
        266777    4 headings,   214 words  -> ALL in notes
        331285    3 headings,   202 words  -> ALL in notes
        103030    3 headings,   199 words  -> ALL in notes
        334494    3 headings,   190 words  -> ALL in important_info
        728042    4 headings,   181 words  -> ALL in important_info

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    IMPORTANT INFORMATION:
    Congratulations on advancing your diving skills and knowledge!
    Before the course starts there is some e-learning for you to complete before the course
    starts. Please create an account on TDISDI website here:
    https://portal.tdisdi.com/register? and email us to let us know this is done so we can
    assign you the code for the e-learning. You can then login to your SDI account and enroll
    by going to 'My elearning' and 'pending enrollments'. Happy learning!
    We will send you another email with more information closer to the time.
    **What to bring**
    Please bring with you all equipment (unless hired), bathers, towel, warm clothes and a
    change of clothes, and ensure you complete the online learning content prior to arriving.
    Let us know if you have any questions.
    **Requirements**
    Before coming in for your Wreck course, please have a read through the SDI medical form.
    If you answer yes to any questions on this form you will need a doctor to sign the
    medical prior to coming in for your dives. Please ensure you read this far enough in
    advance to allow for making an appointment if necessary.
    [View SDI Medical.pdf](https://cdn.filestackcontent.com/1KPKfvFBSpuOdwMuRCFJ)

  COMMENT   : 

  AUTOMATED FINDINGS  (unverified)
    * URL LOST -- a link in the raw reached no column
        - https://www.experiencekart.co.nz/
    * URL ALTERED -- a link was changed or invented
        - https://www.experiencekart.co.nz/.

  RECOVERED CONTENT -- reached no column (heading: text)
        Tour Requirements: Ngā mihi nui,

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Kia Ora!
    Thank you for booking with ExperienceKart, we’re really looking forward to hosting you.
    We hope you enjoy the experience as much as we do.
    Below are a few important details to ensure everything is well prepared for your
    experience.
    We kindly ask that you review this information prior to travel and keep your voucher
    accessible during your experience.
    Should you have any specific preferences or requirements, please feel free to let us
    know.
    If you have any questions at any time, please feel free to reach out, we’re here to help.
    # Departure Information
    - Thank you for selecting your pickup location. We'll reconfirm your exact pickup time
    closer to your travel date to ensure everything runs smoothly.
    - Please be ready at your designated location at least 10 minutes before the scheduled
    time on the day of your tour.
    - If you have not confirmed any hotel pick up with us, or any changes to your pick-up
    location must be confirmed with us with at least 24 hours prior to your tour departure.
    - Please call us or contact us by email to confirm. You will find our contact details in
    this confirmation email.
    # General Information
    - Tours are suitable for people of all ages and average fitness. Our scenic walks are
    short and optional. Please let our guide know at check in if you have any fitness
    concerns.
    - Swimwear can be purchased directly at the venue, subject to availability.
    - Guests are advised to shower before entering the pools in order to remove any makeup,
    sunscreen or other products applied to the skin as we cannot confirm what is in each
    product and therefore if a reaction with the Sulphur will occur.
    - The list of medical conditions below highlight some of the main medical conditions
    where the geothermal pools may not be advised: Pregnancy, Low or High Blood Pressure,
    Mobility Problems, Active or Under Active Thyroid, Verruca/ Warts, Heart Conditions,
    Epilepsy, Skin diseases/disorders, Diabetes, Metal Pins/ Plates, Claustrophobia, Ear
    Infections, Cancer, Recent Surgery, or Sulphur Allergies.
    # What to bring
    - Comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions; warm layered clothes work best.
    - A comfortable shoes with a good grip.
    - Bathing attire.
    - Sunscreen / Sunglasses / Sun hat.
    - Bring your camera to click the best shots.
    - A light rain jacket (just in case).
    - Essential personal items.
    # Tour Requirements
    - Minimum age is 16 years.
    - Please wear comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions, walking shoes, sun
    protection, jacket/warm layer, bring a camera & any essential personal items.
    - This is a self-guided walking tour and sensible walking footwear is recommended.
    - Being an active geothermal area with uneven terrain, sturdy footwear is recommended.
    Jandals, sandals, flip-flops or high heeled shoes are not suitable.
    - Easy and good fitness level required.
    - You must be able to climb and descend stairs and walk on unpaved or uneven terrain.
    - If the guest has had or is suffering from any of the medical conditions, we strongly
    advise that they seek advice from their doctor prior to using the geothermal pools.
    - It is important to drink water while bathing in order to keep hydrated.
    - Please remove all jewellery before entering the pools as no responsibility is taken for
    tarnished jewellery.
    We look forward to having you aboard!
    Ngā mihi nui,
    ExperienceKart
    Phone: 0800 EXP KART | 0800 397 527 | +64 21 026 60993 (Outside NZ)
    Office Hours: 07:00am - 07:00pm (Mon - Sun)
    Email: bookings@experiencekart.co.nz
    Website: https://www.experiencekart.co.nz/

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    **What's included**
    * Safety Briefing and paddling instruction.
    * Guide.
    * Splash pants and jackets.
    * PFD (personal Flotation Device).
    * Wetshoes provided.
    * Changing area and secure storage available.
    * Dry bag to store personal items in while kayaking.
    * Warm woollen socks (during Winter)
    * Plenty of parking onsite.
    * Your own personal guide
    * Upgraded gear
    * Kayak backrest
    * A platter with local cheeses, chocolates and fruit
    **Itinerary**
    **Arrive & check-in**– Arrive at our base and check in and get fitted with your gear. You
    will be sent a text message the day of your tour confirming the exact time of check in.
    **Safety briefing & setup** – Meet your guides for a safety briefing and basic paddling
    instruction before heading out.
    **Paddle across Lake Karapiro** – Begin with a gentle paddle along the lake edge, passing
    the submerged Horahora Dam while your guide shares local history.
    **Enter the Pokaiwhenua Stream** – As the light fades, paddle into the sheltered canyon
    surrounded by native bush and birdlife. Continue upstream (approx. 1–1.15 hours / 1.5km)
    **Stretch & explore** – There’s an opportunity to step out of your kayak, stretch your
    legs, and hear more about the history of the area before continuing. Stop for a 30-minute
    break.
    **Glow worm experience** –Once darkness falls, retrace your path as you drift quietly
    back down the stream beneath a stunning display of glow worms lighting up the canyon
    walls.
    **Return to base** – Continue your relaxed paddle back to base, taking in the peaceful
    night-time atmosphere.(approx. 45 minutes). covering around 3km in total.
    **What to bring**
    * Weather-appropriate clothing (layers recommended).
    * Camera for photos while upstream in daylight.
    **Special requirements**
    * Minimum age is 8 years; all children under 15 must be accompanied by a capable adult or
    guardian; ratio of 1 adult per child.
    * Please advise us in advance of any medical conditions, injuries, or mobility concerns.
    * A reasonable level of fitness is required.
    * All participants must follow safety instructions and wear provided safety equipment
    where required.
    * Evening temperatures drop during the return downstream.
    **Restrictions**
    * No jeans (they get very cold when wet)
    * Flash photography after dark is not allowed as it spoils viewing of glow worms for
    yourself and others. You can use cameras going upstream while its still light.
    * Minimum age is 8 years. All children must be accompanied by an adult or guardian, with
    a ratio of 1 adult per child.
    If you do not meet this ratio (e.g. 1 adult with 2 children), please get in touch—an
    additional guide can be arranged to accompany a child at an extra cost.
    * Advertised weight limit for kayaking is 120kg.
    **NOTE: Riverside Adventures has zero alcohol & smoking policy on Glow Worm Tours.**
    **Disclaimers**
    * Bookings are essential and minimum participant numbers apply.
    * This is a guided outdoor activity and operates in most weather conditions.
    * All participants must be able to understand and follow safety instructions delivered in
    English.
    * If participants are unable to understand English, they must arrange their own
    translator to accompany them.
    * Riverside Adventures Waikato reserves the right to refuse participation if safety
    instructions cannot be clearly understood.
    * Participants must be physically able to paddle.
    * If, after the initial paddle on Lake Karapiro, a participant is deemed unable to safely
    kayak, they will be guided back to base and no refund will be given.
    * Late arrivals may miss the safety briefing and may not be able to join the tour.
    * No refunds will be given for no-shows or late arrivals.

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    carefully about your upcoming stay.
    - Please check your reservation statement to ensure you have booked the correct dates.
    Amendments can be made 24 hours or more before the date of arrival.
    - Check in time is from 2pm - 7:30pm. Strictly no arrivals before 2pm unless you have
    pre-purchased the early check in option when booking.
    - Check out time is strictly 11am.
    - This is a Family Friendly Complex. In the event of excess noise after 9pm (21.00hrs )
    or anti social behavior occurring, your campsite occupants will be asked to leave.
    - We do not have a dump station and the nearest one to us is in either Rotorua or Taupo.
    Please plan to dump your greywater before or after staying with us.
    - Cellphone reception is very limited, unless your provider is One (Vodafone).
    - Wifi is available for purchase at reception ($5 for 2GB).
    - Please ensure that your vehicle or tent fits comfortably within the dimensions stated
    in the description of the site that you booked.
    - With the exception of certified safety dogs (documentation required), pets are not
    permitted in the campground during long weekends or busy periods. If you would like more
    information about this, please email us at info@hotpools.co.nz
    - We are a small campground with limited facilities. Please bring your own cooking
    utensils as we do not provide these.
    - We are approximately 30 minutes from a supermarket. We recommend buying any essentials
    prior to arrival.
    - Our washing machine and dryer take $2 coins. It is $4 per wash and $4 per dry.
    - Peak season rates apply for long weekends and holiday periods. Please email
    info@hotpools.co.nz for more information.
    - Please ensure you have read and understood our [complex
    rules](https://www.hotpools.co.nz/about/health-safety/) and [terms &
    conditions](https://www.hotpools.co.nz/about/terms-conditions/) before your stay with us.
    - There will be a reminder message sent to you closer to your arrival date. Please take
    note of the additional information it entails.

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    ##General Tips
    You will get wet from splashes and you will get even wetter if you fall in! We recommend
    wearing swimming costumes or light weight shorts and a t-shirt. Shoes are not necessary.
    If you wear sunglasses we recommend having a safety strap on them.
    Please note that we do not take any responsibility for loss or damage of your belongings.
    **Bring with you:**
    - Towel for the end
    - Dry change of clothes if you wish to do so
    - Sun protection – Hat/sunglasses/sunscreen
    - A water bottle each
    - Any medications that may be required e.g. Asthma Inhaler, Epipen etc.
    …And an attitude for adventure!!
    ##What to expect
    We will be meeting just before the start time on the date you are booked in at the main
    carpark at Wagga Beach. From there we will get you kitted out with a PFD and then carry
    our paddle boards down to the waters edge on the sand where we will have a safety
    briefing and instruction on use. We will then have a practice and play out on the water!
    Once confident we set off on our 2km all downstream journey and arrive at the Wiradjuri
    Reserve boat ramp where there will be a bus waiting to pick us up and take us back to the
    beach!
    ##Kayak Tour Conditions:
    All prices included are inclusive of GST.
    No refund will be given for bookings cancelled within 24 hours of scheduled tour
    departure.
    In Motion Fitness Kayak Tours & Hire reserves the right to cancel tours at short notice
    due to unsuitable weather/river condition or any other reason. If a cancellation occurs,
    In Motion Fitness Kayak Tours & Hire will attempt to re-schedule. If the tour cannot be
    rescheduled a full refund will be provided.
    Our tours can be short through to long distance, and involve paddling within exposed
    marine surroundings ie: possible wind, currents etc. In Motion Fitness Kayak Tours & Hire
    endeavours to provide a safe experience, as easy going as possible however, all
    participants must acknowledge this BEFORE PROCEEDING and be confident of their fitness
    levels and adventurous nature before deciding to participate.
    All Adults MUST have swimming ability despite being provided with a PFD. It is preferred
    that children have swimming ability and/or are confident within a marine environment.

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Your upcoming adventure will immerse you in one of the Northern Territory's most iconic
    Aboriginal cultural festivals, combined with authentic, guided experiences.
    ##Your Itinerary:
    ######Departure from Katherine: June 7th, 2025
    ######Return to Darwin: June 9th, 2025
    ###Your Barunga Festival Cultural Package includes:
    - Round-trip transportation from Katherine to Barunga and return
    - Full entry to the vibrant Barunga Festival
    - Exclusive Dalabon Experience with Manuel Pamkal
    - All meals during the package (except breakfast on Day 1 and afternoon tea on Day 3)
    - Comfortable swag camping at the Barunga Festival grounds (single or double
    configuration)
    ###Important Information:
    This experience requires a minimum number of participants to proceed.
    A $100 non-refundable booking fee applies to all reservations.
    An initial deposit, including the $100 booking fee and an additional $400, is due at

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Kia ora,
    Thank you for making a group booking at the Waikite Valley Hotpools. Please read this
    email carefully about your upcoming visit.
    - Please Click Here to download our [RAMS
    form](https://www.hotpools.co.nz/media/mhpjxpot/rams-march-2025-pdf.pdf).
    - Opening hours are 10:30am - 8pm and the last entry to the pools is 7pm. As a group, we
    advise that you arrive no later than 6pm.
    - If you are travelling by cars or vans, continue to the end of our driveway to locate
    our main carpark. If you are travelling by bus, please park in the alternative carpark on
    the left as you first enter our driveway. If there is not sufficient space for your bus
    in the alternative carpark, you can park on the left hand side of our driveway as you
    first enter the premise and then carefully walk with your group to reception. Please do
    not park in the campground or staff carpark.
    - Given that this is a water-based activity, we require a ratio of 1:2 (Adults : Children
    4 years old and under) and 1:5 (Adults: Children 5 - 11 years old).
    - Active supervision at all times is the responsibility of the group organiser/leader.
    Active supervision means watching the child at all times and being able to provide
    immediate help. The adult/s must be close enough to see the child, be seen by the child,
    hear the child, be heard by the child and reach the child immediately if they need help.
    - All members of your group will need to be present at reception before you access the
    pools.
    - There is free shelving available or lockers that require a $2 coin for up to 3 hours.
    - We do not offer Wifi and the surrounding cellphone reception is very limited unless
    your provider is One NZ (Vodafone).
    - Our snack cafe is open from 11:30am - 6pm. These hours are subject to change.
    - We have drinking water, swimming nappies, hire towels and hire adult swimsuits
    available at reception.
    - No outside food is to be brought or consumed on cafe terraces and/or in the cafe. All
    picnic food is required to be eaten on the picnic tables by the Settlers Pool which is an
    uncovered area. Use of the cafe area and tables for picnic food will not be permitted if
    it rains during your visit.
    - We do not allow BBQ's or other cooking equipment.
    - Access to our Eco Trail is included in your entry fee so be sure to check it out.
    - Please ensure you have read and understood our [complex
    rules](https://www.hotpools.co.nz/about/health-safety/) and [terms &
    conditions](https://www.hotpools.co.nz/about/terms-conditions/) before your visit.
    - Please ensure that members of your group understand the rules prior to visiting. This
    is to ensure the enjoyment of everyone.
    - Proof of address and photo ID will be required at reception for local group bookings.
    - Please be sure to read and understand our cancellation policy which is also included
    email.
    GST TAX INVOICE
    GST# 88-952-413

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    To ensure the most seamless experience, please familiarize yourself with the information
    below.
    ### Meeting Place
    The meeting place will be at The Enormous Crocodile and Shake Bar. Please be punctual for
    your booking as no extension will be provided.
    ### Food and Drinks
    We will have a table set up at the Enormous Crocodile and Shake Bar for you to use.
    Please note, it is self catered, we do not offer any sort of catering service. You may
    bring party food, or order food to be delivered. We have ice-cream cake available for
    order at $55, please inform us 5 working days in advance should you be keen to order a
    cake. The flavours available are: Oreo, Unicorn, Hokey Pokey.
    ### Croc Bike Capacity
    There is a maximum capacity for Croc bikes:
    - 8 paddlers and 4 adults, or
    - up to 16 paddlers and 6 adults
    Additional Croc Bikes - $29 for 3-seaters, $44 for 6-seaters
    ### Event Flow
    When you arrive at the Shake Bar on the day, you will be given a space to personalise.
    Your group will depart at the party start time, the Shake Bar staff will get you on the
    Croc Bikes and head on to Fergs Wellington! Your kayak/SUP instructor meet you directly
    at Fergs Wellington, and will run through a welcome and health and safety talk. If any of
    your party-goers are late, you can get them to head directly to Fergs Wellington.
    At Fergs Wellington, we will begin with a quick paddle lesson. Thereafter, you will get
    on the water for some splashing great fun!
    You will have to leave Fergs Wellington at least 15 min before your party end time to
    return the Croc bikes to Shake Bar. If you want more time for party festivities, we
    reccommend leaving eariler.
    ###Attire
    Please ensure all participants are wearing suitable clothing for paddling. We recommend
    shorts and a t-shirt or togs with warm layers underneath (such as thermals or merino).
    Please ensure all participants bring a towel and a warm, dry change of clothes for after,
    as they could get wet while on the water.
    We provide all the required technical equipment (spray jackets, PFDs and paddles).
    Thank you again, and we look forward to hosting you.
    Nga mihi,
    The Fergs Team

    Cancellations must be made a minimum of 60 days prior to the commencement of the hire for

    will only be made if the same boat has been hired for the same period at the same price.

    your booking. Travel insurance is recommended for Houseboat Bookings incase you need to
    alter your travel plans.

    to hire due to morons. If you try and play games to avoid this we have the right to

    - Return - 7:00 pm

    Unless we advise otherwise our crew will meet you for boarding at the Marina glass door
    entrance, next to Lolas Pagola, Cullen Bay.
    ![Description of image](https://cdn.filestackcontent.com/4iVMBMjYQMeihiFIzMBH)
    All cruises that are 2 hrs
    - Check In - 4:50 pm
    - Departure - 5:00 pm
    - Return - 7:00 pm
    _____________
    If you have a cruise with nibbles
    Nibbles = consisting of Two Cheeses, kabana, dip, biscuits, sun dried tomatoes, carrot
    and celery sticks, olives, dried fruit, nuts.
    - BYO Drinks are also welcome as we do not have a bar on board. We prefer clients to
    bring their own small eskies and drinks. We can provide wine glasses. Please advise if
    this your intention.
    - Wear comfortable clothes and flat shoes.
    Our Passenger Safety Note
    Sailing vessels utilise shared spaces for clients and staff. To provide a healthy and
    smoke-free comfortable place for our staff and clients, we have a nonsmoking policy.
    Vapes are allowable provided the user maintains a space around people. We reserve our
    right to refuse permission to smoke and vape.
    The skipper is responsible for the safety of everyone on board. Should any client seem to
    be unruly, disturbing other passengers or affected by excessive alcohol or drugs, and in
    the skipper's opinion marine safety is compromised, the skipper has the authority to
    return to port and disembark the relevant client(s).
    The skipper can refuse boarding for the cruise, if he considers the person is under the
    influence of alcohol or drugs, and in his opinion may endanger the safety of himself,
    crew, the vessel or others.
    Cheers!
    Les
    Liability
    City of Darwin Cruises does not accept any responsibility for self injury, illness or the
    loss or damage to personal articles including mobile phones, cameras, and baggage.
    ______________
    ##Dietary Requirements
    We can accommodate you if you have forgotten to mention it while booking, just give us a
    ring or an email and we will do what we can!
    If we have COVID Restricted Trips
    We would like people to wear a mask during boarding of the vessel. IF people are not part
    of a social/family bubble/group, then masks should be worn on the boat if people cannot
    keep a social distance (1.5 m).

PRODUCT 232556   Private Tour: The Ultimate Canopy Experience

  stratum   : heading_rich   words 613   headings 4

  collapse  : 26% of the words in restrictions   (6 content columns used)

        Tour Requirements: Ngā mihi nui,

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Kia Ora!
    Thank you for booking with ExperienceKart, we’re really looking forward to hosting you.
    We hope you enjoy the experience as much as we do.
    Below are a few important details to ensure everything is well prepared for your
    experience.
    We kindly ask that you review this information prior to travel and keep your voucher
    accessible during your experience.
    Should you have any specific preferences or requirements, please feel free to let us
    know.
    If you have any questions at any time, please feel free to reach out, we’re here to help.
    # Departure Information
    - Thank you for selecting your pickup location. We'll reconfirm your exact pickup time
    closer to your travel date to ensure everything runs smoothly.
    - Please be ready at your designated location at least 10 minutes before the scheduled
    time on the day of your tour.
    - If you have not confirmed any hotel pick up with us, or any changes to your pick-up
    location must be confirmed with us with at least 24 hours prior to your tour departure.
    - Please call us or contact us by email to confirm. You will find our contact details in
    this confirmation email.
    # General Information
    - Tours operate in all weather conditions even if it is raining.
    - All equipment is industry standard and well maintained. Participants are well informed
    and properly equipped before each activity.
    - The forest is usually a few degrees colder than it is in the city and can get quite
    frosty in the winter. We recommend a warm pair of socks and one more layer than what you
    are comfortable in outside.
    - High quality wet weather gear and an extra warm outer layer can be supplied.
    - You will be provided with wet weather gear (if required) & all safety equipment.
    # What to bring
    - Comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions; warm layered clothes work best.
    - A comfortable closed toe footwear with a good grip.
    - Sunscreen / Sunglasses / Sun hat.
    - Please also bring a jersey or coat if it is cold.
    - A light rain jacket (just in case).
    - Bringing your camera is at your own risk, we recommend ensuring that your camera has a
    strap that you can attach to yourself or can be kept in a zip pocket.
    - If you have a GoPro, bring it! You will get some amazing footage, we recommend a helmet
    mount/ head strap, wrist mount or selfie stick. Don’t forget to charge your batteries
    before coming on tour.
    # Tour Requirements
    - Please wear comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions, Sun protection,
    jacket/warm layer, and any essential personal items.
    - Maximum weight limit is 120kg. Please advise if your child weighs less than 35kg.
    - Minimum age of 10 years old. There is no upper age limit – so far the oldest customer
    was 93!
    - Children (15 years and under) must be accompanied by a responsible adult (16 years or
    over).
    - Moderate fitness level required - must have the ability to go for a half day forest
    walk or hill walk.
    - We need all participants to be able to raise both knees to hip height (to fly safely
    into platforms).
    - Ability to walk up 6 flights of stairs unassisted without stopping for a break.
    - If pregnant, we do not recommend participating if you are past 20weeks (something to
    look forward to in a few months!)
    We look forward to having you aboard!
    Ngā mihi nui,
    ExperienceKart
    Phone: 0800 EXP KART | 0800 397 527 | +64 21 026 60993 (Outside NZ)
    Office Hours: 07:00am - 07:00pm (Mon - Sun)
    Email: bookings@experiencekart.co.nz
    Website: https://www.experiencekart.co.nz/

    Your booking for Gold Coast's 3-Hour Photography Course for Beginners is confirmed. This
    course runs from 10:00 am - 1:00 pm. Please arrive at the course at least 5 minutes
    early.
    There will be a 15-minute break around 11:30 am.
    We’ll start our workshop with an introduction to the basics of photography. We will be
    walking around many of Gold Coast's photogenic spots to take photographs throughout the
    course. Lighting and composition are the two most important factors when trying to
    achieve a great photograph; you will learn how to frame a photograph for the best results
    and how different lighting changes the look of your photographs.
    [Click here to download your photography notes as a
    PDF](https://cdn.filestackcontent.com/sZNPs4RhR0WNxFLjSJxo)
    If you would like a hard copy version, please advise by email and I’ll organise this for
    you.
    ##Please bring:
    - Your gift vouchers on your phone or printed out
    - An external flash if your camera does not have an inbuilt one
    - Camera Manual downloaded on your phone or hard copy if handy
    - Digital or Film camera of which you have a basic working knowledge ( if you are using
    film, please let us know ahead of time )
    - One empty 4GB or larger memory card or 2 x 36 rolls of film.
    - Fully charged battery and spares if you have any.
    ##Also recommended:
    - Umbrella (if showers are forecast)
    - All-weather comfortable casual clothing including a rain jacket and comfortable walking
    shoes
    - Hand Sanitiser

    Should you have any specific preferences or requirements, please feel free to let us
    know.
    If you have any questions at any time, please feel free to reach out, we’re here to help.
    # Departure Information
    This is a fully guided private tour, with convenient pickup available from any hotel or
    Airbnb location in Auckland. Please provide your pickup details at the time of your
    booking.
    The operator will confirm your exact pickup time closer to your travel date. To ensure a
    smooth departure, kindly be at your designated pickup location at least 10 minutes before
    the scheduled time.
    # General Information
    - Children below 8 years old are required to travel in a child safety car seat.
    - Children between 5-7 years old are also recommended to be in a booster seat for their
    safety.
    - Tours are suitable for people of all ages and average fitness. Our scenic walks are
    short and optional. Please let our guide know at check in if you have any fitness
    concerns.
    - We can accommodate a stroller or a pram, provided it is fully collapsible and can fit
    in the luggage compartment of our buses.
    - Licensed bar available at Te Puia, drinks available for purchase at an additional cost.
    - All visitor guide material for valley walks and boat cruises are printed in the
    following languages: Dutch, German, Chinese, English, Italian, Japanese, Hebrew, Russian,
    Korean, Mandarin, French, Spanish, Portuguese, Hindi, and Slav.
    - Guests are advised to shower before entering the pools to remove any makeup, sunscreen
    or other products applied to the skin as we cannot confirm what is in each product and
    therefore if a reaction with Sulphur will occur.
    - You must be at least 18 years of age and present a valid photo ID to consume alcohol.
    # What to bring
    - Comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions; warm layered clothes work best.
    - A comfortable shoes with a good grip.
    - Sunscreen / Sunglasses / Sun hat.
    - Bring your camera/GoPro to click the best shots.
    - A light rain jacket (just in case).
    - Essential personal items.
    # Tour Requirements
    - Please wear comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions, walking shoes, sun
    protection, jacket/warm layer & essential personal items.
    - Good and medium fitness level required.
    - This is a guided walking tour and sensible walking footwear is recommended.
    - You must be able to climb and descend stairs and walk on unpaved or uneven terrain.
    - Bring a camera or a phone with camera capability as this is the perfect place to get
    some great travel photos.
    - You must be at least 18 years of age and present a valid photo ID in order to consume
    alcohol.
    - If the guest has had or is suffering from any of the medical conditions, we strongly
    advise that they seek advice from their doctor prior to using the geothermal pools.
    - It is important that children (as well as adults) drink water while bathing to keep
    hydrated.
    - Please remove all jewellery before entering the pools as we accept no responsibility
    for tarnished jewellery.
    - For the bathing experience, guests can choose to bring their own bathing attire or hire
    these items from Polynesian Spa at a small cost.
    We look forward to having you aboard!
    Ngā mihi nui,
    ExperienceKart
    Phone: 0800 EXP KART | 0800 397 527 | +64 21 026 60993 (Outside NZ)
    Office Hours: 07:00am - 07:00pm (Mon - Sun)

    You have just booked an unforgettable adventure with Trek Ningaloo. You will love this
    magnificent part of Australia, and we can't wait to show you what it's all about.
    To ensure you get the most out of your Trek Ningaloo experience, please take the time to
    read through the information attached below. If you have any questions about your trek,
    and you cannot find your answer on our FAQ’s page of our website, then please contact us
    either by email or phone, and we will get back to you as soon as we possibly can.
    If you will be hiring our Range Ready equipment package, all you have to do is turn up on
    the day with the Items as listed below, in a small soft bag, that is everything in the
    “What clothing do I need?” and “What else should I bring?” lists. Please be dressed
    appropriately, as listed below, and ready to hit the trail.
    If You have opted to bring your own equipment, as well as the “What clothing do I need?”
    and “What else should I bring?” lists, please ensure you have brought everything as
    recommended in the “Equipment List”.
    Your Guide will pick you up from aproximately 07.00hrs onwards (we’ll call to confirm
    pick up time), please be ready and waiting at the front of your accommodation.
    Ensure you have had breakfast and have begun to hydrate. Aim to drink at least 2 litres
    of water before commencing your trek.
    Once all trekkers have been picked up we will head to our rendezvous point, where we will
    distribute, fit and check over our equipment. Once we’re all squared away, we will head
    to our trek start point for a final safety brief, then take our first steps up onto the
    range. Happy Trekking!
    **Important information regarding your trek please download.**
    [View Information regarding your
    trek.pdf](https://cdn.filestackcontent.com/JNcFkQcxRfWHc39d4rOw)
    ### Trek Notes
    This is an **extremely challenging trek.** A **very high level of physical and mental
    fitness and endurance** is required. This is a full pack, four-day expedition; you will
    be trekking in some extremely rough trying terrain. You must be capable of walking over
    20km per day with a heavy pack for up to 8, sometimes 10 hours a day.
    [View Get Trek-Fit Training
    schedule.pdf](https://cdn.filestackcontent.com/oKWuTqNxQq2Z3LTXiOAP)
    Note: This itinerary may be subject to change due to natural events and upon instruction
    from the Department of Biodiversity, Conservation & Attractions (DBCA) due to Park
    management restrictions.
    #Note:
    **Please download and fill in the Medical History form below and bring a copy with you on
    the day of your trek. If required or you're over 60, please also have the Medical
    Clearance form signed and emailed back to us before the start of your trek.**
    [View Medical History & Medical
    Clearance.pdf](https://cdn.filestackcontent.com/gH03Q7EORhOionovN3fj)

    ###Reminder
    Please ensure that this booking confirmation is circulated to all those that you have
    booked for. Please also ask them all to read the important information, tour rules and
    health and safety information included.
    ###OUR MOST IMPORTANT (*but not our only*) TOUR RULES:
    • **Guests must be wearing flat-soled enclosed shoes.** Thongs, sandals and high heels
    will not be permitted on tour.
    • **Each guest is required to bring their own torch.** Torch apps on smart phones are
    suitable. Torches must be turned on when directed to move, however they can be turned off
    when stopped for atmosphere. Anyone found to be deliberately flashing torches into
    another's face will be immediately removed from the tour.
    • We believe the weather really adds to the atmosphere of a tour. **If there is rain,
    please bring an umbrella and or raincoat, and enjoy the tour.** Tours will only be
    cancelled if conditions become dangerous - we will notify you in these circumstances
    close to the tour time.
    • Please bring a water bottle with you to keep hydrated during 2 hr walking tour.
    • Please arrive 15 minutes before your tour is due to start.
    ---
    ###Accessibility
    [CLICK HERE for accessibility
    information](https://ghosttoursaustralia.com.au/disability-access-companion-cards/)
    ###Suitability
    Paranormal Investigations are not suitable for anyone aged 17 years or younger (unless
    otherwise stated).
    ###Reminder of our Tour Rules/ Company Policy:
    By undertaking one of our tours, all clients agree to abide by the following rules. Any
    person not abiding by the rules will not be permitted on the tour. No refund or transfer
    will be granted.
    • There are **no toilet facilities** available at site. Please use facilities elsewhere
    before joining us at this site.
    • The consumption of alcohol and use of illegal substances are not permitted BEFORE or
    DURING a tour, any guest believed to be intoxicated, inebriated, or otherwise under the
    influence of drugs and/or alcohol will not be admitted at time of check-in.
    • Smoking/ vaping is not permitted at any time during our tours. If anyone forgets this,
    and starts smoking, they will be asked to immediately cease. If they do not, they will be
    asked to leave the tour.
    • Proof of age or other identification may be required upon request for clients claiming
    concessions.
    • Once tours have commenced, guests will be unable to leave the tour group. Visitors must
    stay with the tour group at all times for their own safety
    • Historic Australia reserves the right to change a tour itinerary and/or Tour Guide(s)
    without notice.
    • Mobile and tablet devices are only to be used on tour whilst taking photographs or in
    an emergency. If you use your phone/ tablet for any other purpose during any of our
    tours, you will be asked to stop. Refusal to do so may result in you being asked to leave
    the tour.
    • Video cameras and recording devices of any sort are NOT permitted.
    • All tours, stories, tales etc are the intellectual property of Jack Sim and Historic
    Australia and may not be recorded or reproduced in any form without written permission
    prior to the commencement of a tour.
    • Horror costumes/ makeup/ masks are not permitted to be worn. Historic Australia
    reserves the right to refuse admission to anyone wearing a horror costume that is deemed
    inappropriate or distasteful.
    • Solo paranormal investigation / ghost hunting is strictly *not* permitted on this tour.
    This includes but is not limited to: apps on mobile devices, Ouija boards, pendulums,
    video and still photography, psychic, medium and clairvoyant channelling of spirits.
    • Certified guide, hearing, assistance and companion animals are the **only** animals
    permitted on tours.
    • Management reserves the right to refuse admission for any reason.
    • Guests engaging in inappropriate behaviour will be removed and not be entitled to a
    refund.
    • Historic Australia reserves the right to refuse entry to any child or person whom is
    deemed to be at risk from the material presented.
    • Guests engaging in criminal offences such as stealing, deliberately damaging or
    vandalising sites will be removed and may face prosecution.
    • Historic Australia accepts no responsibility for lost or stolen property.
    • Knives, sharp implements, weapons, pens, pencils, permanent markers, glass bottles,
    cans, hot food, are strictly not permitted during a tour.
    • Loitering at sites at the conclusion of tour is not permitted.
    • Undertaking a Historic Australia Tour is at your own risk.
    Please always:
    • Respect the role/authority of the tour guides.
    • Respect other client’s experience.
    • Respect the historic sites visited.

    ##Dear Guest
    Thank you for booking a Learn to Sea Kayak - Sea Starter Course.
    Please make sure you are familiar with our Terms & Conditions and everyone on your
    booking has completed the **SmartWaiver** prior to the day before your course.
    [https://waiver.smartwaiver.com/w/rrc6scsvvrulyr9hmdkd6c/web/](https://waiver.smartwaiver.com/w/rrc6scsvvrulyr9hmdkd6c/web/)
    **Clothing**
    - Thermals, fleece top, board shorts / thermal long pants (depending on conditions),
    - Footwear (can be old runners, but not thongs, sandals or crocs)
    - Light rain jacket (summer and winter)
    - Sun glasses with retainer and hat are a must!
    - Wetsuits are a good idea in the colder months (we have some you can use 0 sleeveless
    are best
    - Spare thermals & raincoat are useful when off the water.
    During the course you will learn a lot about paddling clothing! See our Youtube:
    [What do I wear](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kgp8VTIQP8U&t=124s)
    Most of these items are available to purchase at our [Kayak
    Shop](https://kayakshopaustralia.com.au/kayak-clothing/) - you can click & collect on the
    day. Allow time to try clothing on so that you have the correct size.
    **Meet** at our Training Centre at The Kayak Shop, 37 Jetty Road Sandringham at 8:30am
    for a 9:00am start.
    **Parking** - a parking fee applies in all car parks. Free if you have a Bayside Council
    Beach Parking Permit. If you wish to park for free, arrive early and drop off your
    personal belongings at the training centre and park your car in Arthur Street, which runs
    parallel with Beach Road, a short 10 min walk back.
    **Lunch** - bring a cut lunch or splash out at the cafe next door - they do a great
    burger!
    ##Duration: 9:00am (arrive 8:30am) to approx. 4:30pm for 2 days (Saturday & Sunday of the
    same weekend)
    We look forward to seeing you. If you have any questions please contact us.
    Kind regards,
    East Coast Kayaking and Sea Kayak Australia

    We offer a delicious selection of platters for adults who are attending the party, if
    you’d like more information, you can find them
    [here](https://www.yarraps.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2022/10/Adult-platters.pdf).
    You can find the allergen information for the children’s party food
    [here](https://www.yarraps.com.au/wp-content/uploads/2022/10/Kids-parties-ingredients-list-2022.pdf).
    Please note some important information for our parties:
    • **We do not supply candles or lighters**, but you are welcome to bring your own for the
    birthday cake. We do sell matches in our shop. For safety reasons, we cannot allow
    sparklers in any of our carriages.
    • We don't supply decorations in our party rooms, bringing focus to the rustic aspect of
    our 100-year-old carriages. You are very welcome to bring you own decorations, but please
    be aware that **we do not allow blu-tac, sticky-tape, poppers, confetti, pinatas or
    glitter**. Any party found to be in violation of this will be charged a fee of $50.
    • You have access to our party room and a party concierge for the first hour of your
    party. The party concierge will show you to your carriage, serve the party food and cake,
    keep the children entertained for the hour and organise the start of your chosen
    activity. **The party concierge is not there to supervise the children during their
    activity**.
    • The person booking the party is required to be on-site for the duration of the party
    and is responsible for the party children.
    • The birthday child is counted as one of the booked number of children.
    • Where possible we will accommodate adults in the party room, but if space does not
    allow, a table will be provided either outside the party room or inside the café.
    • A $50 surcharge is payable for parties held on a public holiday.
    • With the exception of Birthday Cakes, we do not allow outside food or drinks.
    #We need the final numbers, catering requests and all other details for the party on the
    Tuesday before the event. We can organise this with you on our site or over the phone.
    You will not be able to change or add to your booking after this Tuesday. We do not
    invoice for parties.
    If you have any questions regarding your party, don’t hesitate to ask 😊
    Kind Regards,
    Yarralumla Play Station
    6282 2714

    ##Arrive 10 minutes prior to your booking.
    To Whom It May Concern,
    **Escapade & Co, Solicitors**
    2/61 Curtis Street
    Ballarat Central, Victoria
    27 September 1892
    _To whom it may concern,_
    It is with due formality and a measure of solemn duty that I write to inform you of the
    passing of Mr. William Parker Inman, proprietor of The Diggers Hotel and a long‑standing
    figure of note within the Ballaarat district. Mr. Inman departed this life peacefully at
    his residence in Golden Point on the evening of the 24th of September, in the year of
    1892, at sixty‑three years of age.
    As you may be aware, Mr. Inman was among the earliest arrivals to the goldfields during
    the great rush of the 1850s. His endeavours as both miner and publican left an indelible
    mark upon the township, and his establishment, The Diggers Hotel, has long served as a
    gathering place for travellers, tradesmen, and townsfolk alike. Though he leaves behind
    no known kin, he was regarded by many as a friend, confidant, and steadfast supporter of
    honest labour.
    In accordance with his final instructions, and by the authority vested in me as executor
    of his estate, you are hereby **requested to attend the formal reading of Mr. Inman’s
    last will and testament**. This proceeding will take place in our offices at 2/61 Curtis
    Street, Ballaarat.
    Mr. Inman’s affairs, though modest in appearance, are of a nature that necessitates the
    presence of all individuals named or otherwise implicated in his final bequests. It is my
    professional obligation to advise that your attendance is considered essential, as
    certain matters of significance—some pertaining to Mr. Inman’s long‑held beliefs
    regarding undiscovered gold within the district—are to be disclosed only at that time.
    Should you require further clarification prior to the gathering, you may direct
    correspondence to my office. Otherwise, I trust you will honour the wishes of the late
    Mr. Inman by presenting yourself promptly at the appointed hour.
    With Kind Regards,
    **Edwin J. McArthur**
    Solicitor and Executor of the Estate of
    Mr. William Parker Inman
    ###Tax Invoice:
    Escapade Escape Rooms Ballarat
    ABN: 62 308 717 077

    must! An insulated rain jacket will keep you warm and dry and be sure to bring long
    socks.
    2. Equipment: If you have your own skates, feel free to bring them along. If not,
    Iceworld provides rental skates free of charge for your convenience. Please arrive early
    to your lesson in order to obtain rental skates if needed and be prepared to step onto
    the ice at your scheduled lesson time.
    3. Safety First: Skating can be a thrilling experience, but safety is our utmost
    priority. Always follow the instructions of your instructors and be mindful of your
    surroundings on the ice. We strongly recommend that young skaters wear a well-fitted
    bike, ice hockey or ski helmet.
    4. Progression: As you embark on your skating journey, remember that progress takes time
    and practice. Be patient with yourself and enjoy the learning process. Our experienced
    instructors are here to guide you every step of the way.
    If you have any questions or need further information about the Aussie Skate™ School
    Program, please don't hesitate to contact us at skateschool@iceworld.com.au. Our team
    will be more than happy to assist you.

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    ###We are delighted to have you join us on this exciting journey into the underwater
    world.
    ###Please review the following important information to ensure a smooth and enriching
    experience during your diving course:
    ###Pre-requisites and Requirements:
    Please ensure that you meet the minimum age and health requirements for the course.
    Complete and sign all necessary medical and liability forms prior to the course start
    date.
    Bring any relevant certification you think will be needed for your course, and any
    required dive logbooks or documents for verification.
    ###What to Bring:
    Swimwear, towel, and comfortable clothing suitable for diving activities.
    Your own diving equipment (if you have it), including mask, snorkel, fins, and wetsuit.
    If you do not have your own equipment, it will be provided during the course.
    Sunscreen, hat, and sunglasses for sun protection during outdoor sessions.
    Water bottle to stay hydrated throughout the course.
    Any personal medication or items you may require during the course.
    ###Important Course Information:
    Please arrive at least 30 minutes prior to the course start time for check-in and
    equipment setup.
    Follow the instructions of our experienced instructors at all times to ensure your safety
    and learning progress.
    Participate actively in all theory sessions, practical exercises, and assessments to
    maximize your learning experience.
    Safety is our utmost priority. Be sure to adhere to all diving protocols and guidelines
    provided by the instructor.
    We are committed to providing you with the highest level of training and ensuring your
    safety and enjoyment throughout the course. If you have any questions or require further
    assistance, please do not hesitate to contact our customer service team. We are here to
    support you on your diving journey.
    We look forward to welcoming you to Gold Coast Dive Centre and helping you discover the
    wonders of the underwater world!
    Best regards,
    Gold Coast Dive Centre

  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    ##Your Lounge Boat Hire is Confirmed!
    **Get ready for the most relaxing seat on the Swan River — your private floating sun
    lounge is waiting**.
    Ahoy Skipper,
    We’re thrilled to have you on board! Soon you’ll be drifting along the beautiful Swan
    River in your own floating sun lounge.
    This isn’t a picnic boat - it’s a barefoot river lounge experience designed for slowing
    down, soaking up the sun and feeling the water around you.
    Before the fun begins, we need to go over a few important things to make sure your day is
    safe, smooth and unforgettable.
    ⚓ **1. Plan Your River Route**
    There are set boundaries for where you can cruise. Get familiar with the Swan River and
    plan your route according to your hire time.
    📍 [See Sample Itineraries & Maps] https://www.nautipicnics.com.au/itinerary/
    Feelin’ Nauti is designed for slow cruising and relaxing
    - Max 4 adults on board
    - Barefoot boat – no shoes allowed (cork floors + sun pad comfort)
    - Sit low to the water — Sun on your face and the Swan River right at your fingertips.
    - Sun pad lounge seating (no picnic table)
    - Towels provided for the sun pad
    - Leave the esky at home and only bring cooler bags, but pack light
    - No glass and please keep alcohol modest – it’s a lounge boat, not a party barge
    More on what to bring: https://www.nautipicnics.com.au/faq/
    👩‍✈️ **2. Choose Your Skipper**
    Even without a license, your skipper is legally responsible for the boat and everyone on
    board. **Forward this email to the skipper if it’s not you**.
    Your skipper must:
    - Understand river rules
    - Check weather conditions
    - Stay under 0.05% blood alcohol
    - Stay alert and in control at all times
    - Read and download the following:
    📚 Skipper Info & Boat Safety https://www.nautipicnics.com.au/boatsafety/
    📱 Track your cruise on Nebo GPS:
    https://fleet.nebo.global/nautipicnics/public/a7e6e560-86e5-4b0c-886a-f94a8eeb5fe5?key=WTR60V5SD7VQ18IJ8X617JUNHE
    🌦 **3. Weather & Refund Protection**
    Perth weather can turn quickly. We do not operate in rain, heatwaves (38°C+), or gusts
    over 35km/h.
    If this is the case on your hire day, please check with us if you are unsure!
    📍 Stay updated: https://www.nautipicnics.com.au/weather-forecast-and-policy
    ➡️ **Coming from out of town or planning something special?**
    Add a $50 Weather Protection.
    If we cancel due to weather, your full hire purchase will be refunded.
    This option can only be added today. Just reply to this email and we’ll charge the card
    on file.
    🚨 **4. Respect the Rules**
    We rarely do this - but we may stop a hire early or refuse boarding if:
    - The skipper isn’t capable or sober
    - Rules are being ignored
    - There’s unsafe or inappropriate behaviour
    This is for everyone’s safety. No refunds will be given if rules are breached and we stop
    the hire early.
    📖 Read Our Safety Manual https://www.nautipicnics.com.au/s-o-m/
    📩 **5. Your Final Reminder Email will dock 48 hours before your hire**
    You’ll receive another email with all the details for the day before your booking.
    **That’s the serious stuff done** ✅
    Whether you're enjoying a relaxed river date, catching up with friends, or stretching out
    on the sun pad — Feelin’ Nauti is all about slowing down and soaking it all in.
    If you have any questions, just reply to this email.
    See you at the marina soon – we can’t wait to welcome you aboard!
    Fair Winds,
    Yvonne
    Nauti Picnics

    Aria Cruises Gold Coast Location:
    📍 95 Marine Parade, Southport, QLD 4215
    (Located at the Aqua Adventure Centre — formerly known as Southport Fishing & Dive
    Centre)
    Look for the Aqua Adventure Centre’s blue shop signage — Aria Cruises operates from the
    marina located behind the shop.
    Please note that Aria Cruises does not accept responsibility for where you park your
    vehicle.
    TIME TO BE THERE
    Please be ready for boarding 10 minutes prior to the time your cruise is scheduled to
    commence.
    THINGS TO BRING
    Things to pack for your cruise include: easy to remove shoes. hat, sunscreen, sunglasses
    and jacket.
    NO BYO DRINKS as we are a licensed venue.
    The boat has drinks available to purchase on board.
    NO LARGE BAGS due to limited space on board.
    Please Note!!!
    You may be required to remove your shoes before boarding, as certain footwear can mark or
    damage the deck. Boat shoes with clean, white rubber soles are permitted. We appreciate
    your understanding and cooperation in helping us keep the vessel in top condition.
    Please remember you will be on a moving vessel with ropes and splashes of saltwater on
    the decks so it's very important that all passengers are aware of what's going on around
    them at all times and that alcohol is consumed responsibly.
    IMPORTANT
    All of our Sailing Cruises are subject to the weather, restrictions and conditions
    therefore THE CRUISE CAN BE POSTPONED AT ANYTIME. Please understand that we don't like to
    cancel but if we do it is because your safety and comfort is important to us and we want
    to offer the best experience possible.
    For further information, photos, terms and conditions please see our website at
    ariacruises.com
    Weather & Sea Sickness Advisory – Whale Watching Cruise
    For your safety and comfort, please be aware that ocean conditions can change quickly.
    Our cruises operate in open waters where wind, swell, and weather may impact the
    smoothness of the journey. Even on clear days, sea conditions can be unpredictable.
    Passengers who are prone to motion sickness are strongly advised to take preventative
    measures prior to boarding. Please consult your healthcare provider for advice on
    suitable options before your trip.
    Please follow crew instructions at all times. If conditions are deemed unsafe, the
    captain may modify or cancel the trip at any time for passenger safety.
    Your wellbeing is our priority, and we appreciate your understanding.
    **By proceeding with a booking and boarding the vessel, all passengers are deemed to have
    accepted Aria Cruises’ Terms and Conditions.**
    These can be viewed [HERE](https://ariacruises.com/terms-and-conditions)
    **The booking holder is solely responsible for ensuring that all accompanying guests are
    fully informed of all relevant information, requirements, conditions, and updates prior
    to boarding.**

    ##Thank you for booking our Muku Women’s Morning Tour.
    Please allow 15 mins window from your pickup time
    ##On the day, please bring
    - An enquiring mind
    - Hat
    - Sunglasses
    - Sunscreen
    - Light, loose clothing
    - Easily removable footwear suitable for short walks on hardened tracks in hot weather
    (enclosed shoes are not really necessary as long as your feet are comfortable and
    protected)
    - Swimming gear (optional).
    There will be an opportunity for a swim in warm, crocodile-safe, natural spring-fed
    pools. Walks will depend on the abilities and interests of the group but the main
    attractions can be reached by short, easy walks. A slightly more strenuous but easy walk
    on a hardened bush tracks up to 1 km is available for those who are keen and able.
    Flies and mosquitoes are not normally a problem on our tours but there may be one or two,
    and possibly a few biting march flies. Please bring repellent if you are particularly
    sensitive to them.
    We'll provide cool, bottled water and brunch.
    If you're keen to do other things while you're here in Darwin, we recommend visiting:
    - [Museum and Art Gallery of the Northern Territory at Fannie
    Bay](http://www.magnt.net.au/)
    - [Darwin Botanic
    Gardens](https://nt.gov.au/leisure/parks-reserves/find-a-park/george-brown-darwin-botanic-gardens)
    and [Eva's Cafe](http://botanicgardenscafe.com.au/)
    - [Mindil Beach Sunset Markets](https://mindil.com.au/) on Thursday and Sunday evenings
    - [Stokes Hill
    Wharf](http://www.waterfront.nt.gov.au/darwin-waterfront-precinct/stokes-hill-wharf/) for
    lunch or dinner— sunset views and a variety of good, inexpensive meals.
    - [Northern Territory Library](https://ntl.nt.gov.au/) at Parliament House
    - [Aquascene](Aquascene) fish feeding at historic Doctor's Gully
    - [Crocodylus Park](Crocodylus Park) at Berrimah
    - [Darwin Military Museum](Darwin Military Museum) at East Point
    - [WWII Oil Storage Tunnels](WWII Oil Storage Tunnels) at Darwin Waterfront
    - [Royal Flying Doctor Service](https://www.rfdsdarwin.com.au/) display at Stokes Hill
    Wharf
    - [Australian Aviation Heritage Museum](https://www.darwinaviationmuseum.com.au/) at
    Winnellie
    - [Territory Wildlife Park](http://www.territorywildlifepark.com.au/) at Berry Springs
    There's a [red hop-on-hop-off
    bus](http://theaustralianexplorer.com.au/darwin-explorer.html) that stops at most of
    these places.
    ###For high end restaurants we'd recommend:
    - Pee Wee's at the Point for seafood and location
    - Hanuman for Thai and Indian dishes
    - Bannsang for Korean dishes
    - Yots for Greek dishes
    - Alfonsino’s for Italian dishes
    - Moorish Cafe for Spanish and North African dishes.
    We look forward to meeting you!
    kind regards
    tess
    Check us out or write a review on [google.com](https://g.page/r/CfigW8l2l839EAg/review)
    or
    [tripadvisor.com.au](www.tripadvisor.com.au/Attraction_Review-g1192207-d1747105-Reviews-Northern_Territory_Indigenous_Day_Tours-Litchfield_National_Park_Northern_Territory.html).

    Please take a moment to read the key details below before your pickup you ride
    #PLEASE NOTE TIMES
    Pick-Up: 8:00am – 11:30am (PICK UP CLOSES AT 11:30am)
    • Drop-Off: By 8:00pm the same day
    • If you’re arriving on the SeaLink Ferry, please catch the 10:30am or earlier service.
    • If returning early, you can leave the key in our drop box.
    #YOUR BOOKING
    You’ve booked a road-registered scooter/moped (50cc) — not an e-bike or e-scooter.
    • It has a throttle and rides in normal traffic up to ~50km/h.
    • All riders must have prior moped or motorbike experience and be 21 or over
    #WHAT TO BRING
    • Open or unrestricted driver’s licence (must be shown on arrival)
    • Ensure all drivers are competent and confident riding a moped
    • It’s hot out there — bring water, sunscreen, and sun protection
    #BEFORE YOU RIDE
    • Please arrive a little early so we can fit your helmet and check your licence.
    • You will need to read the hire contract rules and see the map
    • You will be taken through the rider instructions on how to operate the vehicle
    #DEPOSIT
    • If you have paid by credit card your details are held for any hire contract charges
    • If you paid via Apple Pay, Google Pay or similar a HOLD of $120 will be applied
    • All personal details are held securely by FareHarbor booking app
    #INSTAGRAM
    https://www.instagram.com/magneticislandroaming/

    TIME

    THE BOAT WILL DEPART FROM HUSKISSON WHARF CURRAMBENE ST, HUSKISSON NSW
    PLEASE BE AT THE WHARF READY TO GO WITH ALL YOUR GEAR BY THE START TIME.

    WAIVER](https://www.jotform.com/form/203201698866058) remember to choose the correct date
    and take a screen shot on your phone of the submitted waiver and show it to the reception
    staff at the Dive Shop (or you can do it on the day if you prefer, please bring your
    smartphone to scan the QR code with).
    You will be out on the boat for between 4 to 5 hours, we will have sunscreen, fresh
    water, biscuits, tea, coffee, hot chocolate and soup for the trip but if you want
    anything else you should bring it with you. Also remember to keep hydrated and use sun
    protection out on the water.
    Divers will need to provide their own transport between the shop and the wharf as we do
    not have enough space in our vehicles. Please call us ahead of the trip if this is going
    to be an issue.
    After we get back feel free to come back up to the shop to record the dives or just have
    a chat about what has gone on.
    Remember we are a [SEA SHEPHERD DIVE
    CENTER](https://divejervisbay.com/environmental-policy/) so we don't support any
    behaviour that will negatively impact on the Marine Life on our trips.
    If you have any questions relating to what will happen feel free to call the shop to
    discuss.
    Sea you soon,
    **The Crew**

    SmartWaiver prior to the day before your course.
    [https://waiver.smartwaiver.com/w/rrc6scsvvrulyr9hmdkd6c/web/](https://waiver.smartwaiver.com/w/rrc6scsvvrulyr9hmdkd6c/web/)
    Clothing - Thermals, fleece, board shorts / thermal long pants (depending on conditions),
    footwear (can be old runners, but not thongs, sandals or crocs), light rain jacket
    (summer and winter), sun glasses with retainer and hat are a must! Wetsuits are a good
    idea in the colder months. Spare thermals & raincoat are useful when off the water.
    During the course you will learn a lot about paddling clothing! See our Youtube:
    [What do I wear](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kgp8VTIQP8U&t=124s)
    Most of these items are available to purchase at our [Kayak
    Shop](https://kayakshopaustralia.com.au/kayak-clothing/) - you can click & collect on the
    day. Allow time to try clothing on so that you have the correct size.
    Meet at our Training Centre at The Kayak Shop, 37 Jetty Road Sandringham at 9am for a
    9:30am start. After the weekend (Sunday night or early Monday morning) we will drive to
    Port Welshpool for the second part of the course.
    Parking - a maximum of $18.00 parking fee applies in all day car parks. Free if you have
    a Bayside Council Beach Parking Permit. If you wish to park for free, arrive early and
    drop off your personal belongings at the training centre and park your car in Arthur
    Street, which runs parallel with Beach Road, a short 10 min walk back.
    Lunch - bring a cut lunch or splash out at the cafe next door - they do a great burger!
    ##Duration: 9:30am to approx. 4:30pm for 2 Days, the 3 days in the Wilson's Prom / Snake
    Island / Corner Inlet area
    We look forward to seeing you. If you have any questions please contact us.
    Kind regards,
    East Coast Kayaking and Sea Kayak Australia

    ![Description of image](https://cdn.filestackcontent.com/k6yK6SWIQEimxTbBDoug)
    Thanks for booking your Canoe session with Absolute Outdoors. We just wanted to touch
    base about what to expect and what to bring to your booking.
    ##PLEASE NOTE:
    In order to maximise your time on the water, please make sure that everyone in your group
    has signed our online waiver prior to arrival.
    All minors (under 18 years of age) must be accompanied by a legal guardian for the
    duration of the activity.
    Participants must provide their own transport to the session location.
    ##Where to meet
    Check-in time is 5 minutes before your booking at the Stawell Yacht Club. Located at 566
    Mokepilly Rd, Lake Fyans VIC 3381, The Stawell Yacht Club is approx. 15-minute drive from
    Halls Gap, so allow enough time to arrive. Please let us know if you are running late.
    ##Facilities
    The Stawell Yacht Club has toilets and drinking water available on site. We always
    encourage participants to arrive fed, watered and ready to go but the facilities are
    there if required.
    ##What to Bring
    - Clothing - It can get quite sunny on the water so think SunSmart. Make sure everything
    you plan to wear is quick-drying and avoid cotton.
    - Rashie or other sun-protective shirt
    - Board shorts or bathers
    - Hat
    - Sunglasses - with a strap if you have one!
    - Water shoes, sandals or old runners -something you don’t mind getting wet and won’t
    fall off if you fall in! (no thongs!)
    ##Other things to bring
    - Suncream
    - Water bottle
    - Snacks
    - A positive attitude - While you might fall in, you will have fun!
    - You can bring your phone on your activity - make sure that it is in a waterproof case
    and you do so at your own risk.
    We look forward to seeing you!
    Adrian Manikas
    [absoluteoutdoors.com.au](https://absoluteoutdoors.com.au)
    [info@absoluteoutdoors.com.au](mailto:info@absoluteoutdoors.com.au)
    [1300 526 258‬](tel:+61353564556)

    - A camera capable of manual settings (Mirrorless or DSLR preferred).
    - Extra SD Cards and charged batteries.
    - A standard, wide angle and/or telephoto (zoom) lens.
    - A tripod (essential). If you do not have a tripod, we have them for hire.
    - A Remote control (preferable but not essential).
    - A Polarising filter and/or ND Filters (preferable but not essential). If you do not
    have ND filters, we have them for hire.
    - Old cotton t-shirt, lens cloths, hand towel.
    - Water to stay hydrated.
    - Hat, sunscreen, and sunglasses for UV protection.
    - Appropriate clothing for both wet and dry weather conditions. We recommend comfortable,
    breathable fabrics like cotton and suggest packing a mix of short-sleeved and
    long-sleeved tops, along with pants for versatility.
    - Sturdy, Comfortable Walking Shoes

    you to the event.
    ###COVID19 Guidelines
    The CapturEDU team is committed to ensuring all in-person events are enjoyable and safe.
    In line with this commitment, we kindly ask you to follow these COVID-19 guidelines:
    - Stay home if you are feeling unwell.
    - If you have recently tested positive for COVID-19 or have been in close contact with
    someone who has, please do not attend. You will be eligible for a full refund.
    - Follow government advice on social distancing and hygiene.
    - Cough or sneeze into your elbow or a tissue.
    - If the event is in Australia, we recommend downloading the COVIDSafe app and keeping it
    active during your time at the event.
    - If you test positive for COVID-19 within two weeks after attending, please notify us
    immediately via the email address provided below.

    tables onsite for your use. BBQ Hire available for $20. Please have an adult available to
    cook your food. Please note there is NO SMOKING on the premises.
    Wear your best combat gear, long pants, enclosed footwear (CROCS are not suitable) and a
    hat - you will be wearing sensors on your head for those "head shots". Please complete
    your waiver form ONLINE with your unique link code. Please sent this code to all of your
    players to sign ONLINE. Let's reduce the paper waste!
    If you have any questions, please let us know. Looking forward to the ultimate laser tag
    experience on our unique bush venue at The Salt Ash Weapons Range.
    PS:- Please reply to this email that you have confirmed and understood all booking
    conditions which can be viewed on www.battlezoneplaylive.com.au.
    Thank you and we look forward to an awesome battle at The Salt Ash Weapons Range. Hooah!

    Thank you very much for your booking, we're excited to show you the beauty of Fiordland!
    Your flight departs from our Lakefront Helipad at 79 Lakefront Drive, Te Anau. **Please
    check in with us 20 mins prior to your flight departure time.**
    ##Weather Updates & Travel Information
    The weather we experience in Fiordland National Park can change quickly, therefore if
    flying conditions are not suitable, we may have to reschedule or cancel your flight. We
    will check weather conditions the day before your flight and contact you if we have any
    concerns. Rest assured that tours booked directly with us are fully refundable if there
    is a cancellation due to the weather.
    ##What Should You Bring
    • Sunglasses
    • Closed/covered shoes
    • Warm clothing - it's usually cooler on the mountain tops so wear a couple of layers
    • Camera for those Instagram worthy shots but please no drones or selfie sticks
    • A big smile!
    Please don’t hesitate to get in touch if you have any questions or wish to change your
    booking. We look forward to seeing you soon.
    Nga mihi nui | Many thanks
    From the team at Southern Lakes Helicopters

    Kia Ora!
    Thank you for booking with ExperienceKart, we’re really looking forward to hosting you.
    We hope you enjoy the experience as much as we do.
    Below are a few important details to ensure everything is well prepared for your
    experience.
    We kindly ask that you review this information prior to travel and keep your voucher
    accessible during your experience.
    Should you have any specific preferences or requirements, please feel free to let us
    know.
    If you have any questions at any time, please feel free to reach out, we’re here to help.
    # Departure Information
    This is a fully guided private tour. We can pick you from any hotel or an Airbnb location
    from Auckland.
    The operator will confirm the pick-up time closer to the travel date. Please make sure
    you are present at the pickup location 10 minutes prior to your departure time.
    Any changes to your pick-up location must be confirmed with us with at least 24 hours
    prior to your tour departure. Please call us or contact us by email to confirm. You will
    find our contact details in our confirmation email.
    # General Information
    - Children below 8 years old are required to travel in a child safety car seat.
    - Children between 5-7 years old are also recommended to be in a booster seat for their
    safety.
    - Tours are suitable for people of all ages and average fitness. Our scenic walks are
    short and optional. Please let our guide know at check in if you have any fitness
    concerns.
    - After heavy frosts, areas that don't see the sun can stay icy so please take care.
    - After heavy rain, the boardwalks and tracks may become slippery so please take care.
    - Tour duration, flight times and inclusions may vary due to weather conditions, volcanic
    activity and passenger loadings.
    - Excursion landings are undertaken at your own risk and such landings are at your
    pilot’s sole discretion.
    # What to bring
    - Comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions; warm layered clothes work best.
    - A comfortable closed toe footwear with a good grip.
    - Sunscreen / Sunglasses / Sun hat.
    - Please also bring a jersey or coat if it is cold.
    - Bring your camera to click the best shots.
    - A light rain jacket (just in case).
    - Essential personal items.
    # Tour Requirements
    - Please wear comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions, walking shoes, sun
    protection, jacket/warm layer & essential personal items.
    - Easy and good fitness level required. and you must be able to climb and descend stairs
    and walk on unpaved or uneven terrain.
    - Bring a camera or a phone with camera capability as this is the perfect place to get
    some great experiences photos.
    - You must be able to climb and descend stairs and walk on unpaved or uneven terrain.
    - Suitable for people of all ages and Easy and good fitness level required.
    - Maximum weight limit is 135kg. Please advise if your weights exceeding 135kgs.
    - If an individual passenger weight exceeds 110kgs, surcharges apply.
    - Please dress with an additional layer of clothing (in winter) & closed toe footwear.
    - Always stay on the boardwalks or marked paths.
    - Do not drop or throw items anywhere in the park; apart from making an unsightly mess,
    you may get boiling water splashed back at you.
    - During the summer season, it is essential to have water or liquid refreshment.
    We look forward to having you aboard!
