  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    We are confirming we have received your $500 deposit to lock in your charter date on MV
    Pelagic. Please see your invoice attached and further details below.
    ##Parking:
    - Parking at Z Pier is pay-by-plate. You can pay using the INUGO app or by credit card at
    the machine on site.
    - For those who are not driving Uber/Taxi can drop them off a Z Pier.
    ##What to bring:
    - Lunch, snacks and plenty of refreshments for the day. A couple of beers is also fine
    - Polarised glasses if possible as they help you see in which makes it easier for
    spotting the wildlife
    - Hat and sunscreen
    - Warm jumper/waterproof jacket and wear comfortable non-slip shoes
    - IMPORTANT - SCOPODERM PATCHES. These are fantastic for preventing sea sickness so if in
    doubt it's best to wear one to ensure a great charter experience or at very least use
    another sea sickness remedy like a combination of ginger calm and a antihistamine. Be
    sure not to touch your eyes when applying scopoderm patches and wash your hands
    thoroughly immediately after applying.
    If you need to contact the skipper prior to your charter please call Andrew on 021 309
    448.
    If weather or Covid is an issue we are always happy to postpone to the next available
    opportunity for everyone.
    We look forward to seeing you and your group for an epic “Work up” charter!
    Kind regards,
    Pelagic Charters

  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Please note all accommodation is available for entry no earlier than 4pm.
    Accommodation is self served with the front door being unlocked and all instructions for
    accommodation on the whiteboard in the lounge area.
    You will need to sign a standard waiver prior to journey departure.
    We reserve the right to cancel this journey if the weather or conditions will deem this
    unsafe.
    ##Disclaimer
    We cannot be held responsible for any electronic equipment or personal items that are
    taken on your trip.
    All of our equipment is checked regularly.
    Our liability is not guaranteed due to user error, and other circumstances out of our
    control.
    Although greatly reduced, there is still a small risk of an accident and / or injury.
    Owhango Adventures and / or the guide cannot be held responsible unless great negligence
    was involved on their part.
    ##Safety Responsibility
    Clients are responsible for their own safety management and clients must meet any
    additional costs arising from bad weather, or injury.
    ##Safety Responsibility
    • If weather and river conditions turn bad during your trip, your first option should be
    to wait it out.
    • Clients are required to replace any equipment lost and are liable for the repair cost
    of any equipment damaged.
    • If conditions turn bad, we will allow you to sit it out and canoe / kayak back a day
    late, at no extra cost, if you require extra jet boat or shuttle assistance this will be
    at your cost.
    • Owhango Adventures is a safety audited business, as required by the Adventure Activity
    Regulations.
    • If you are a school/educational group ask us for a copy of our SOP or Rams to help you
    with your planning.
    The minimum recommended age for our trips is 7 years
    If we believe the weather conditions are improving we may delay the departing time, or
    postpone until the following day.
    If your trip is cancelled due to bad weather or river conditions, or, if you are unable
    to postpone until the following day, all amounts held by Owhango Adventures will be
    refunded.
    If you need to transfer to Pipiriki by jet boat you are required to pay for this.
    Some exceptions apply.
    Once you have paid for and / or started a canoe trip you will not be refunded for change
    of mind. If you require extra jet boat or shuttle services you will be charged
    accordingly.

    **Sydney HeliTours**
    _________________________
    ##On The Day
    ***If making your own way, please arrive 30 minutes before your flight time for check-in
    and a safety briefing.***
    - Free parking is available
    - Passengers must notify staff if they have been scuba diving in the past 24 hours
    - No selfie sticks allowed on or around the helicopter
    ##Weight restrictions and aircraft limitations
    Accurate individual passenger weights MUST be provided at the time of booking. Failure to
    provide accurate weights may result in the passenger(s) being unable to fly or further
    costs to the passenger(s) to proceed with the planned flight or aircraft upgrade. This is
    all based on safety and will never be compromised. Sydney HeliTours will take no
    responsibility for any costs associated with the passenger(s) being unable to take their
    flight if inaccurate weights are provided. If passenger(s) are unable to fly due to
    inaccurate weights no refund will be given.

PRODUCT 631977   Guided Kayak Tour
================================================================================================
  stratum   : heading_rich   words 469   headings 1

  headings  : Important Information

  VERDICT   : ____________________   (hand review)
  COMMENT   : 

  AUTOMATED FINDINGS: none fired

  review signal -- a heading named this column but the model routed the content elsewhere (usually correct)
    disclaimers, meeting_point, what_to_bring, cancellation

  RECOVERED CONTENT -- reached no column (heading: text)
        no heading: Looking forward to seeing you soon!

  REWORDED -- present but not verbatim
        no heading: The Manly Sailing Team

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Hi,
    Thank you for booking with Manly Sailing. This email is to confirm we’ve received your
    booking and we look forward to welcoming you for a memorable time out on the water.
    Please note, all of our experiences require a minimum of two participants to go ahead. If
    we do not reach the minimum number for your session, your booking may be subject to
    change. If that happens, we’ll get in touch with you in advance to discuss your options.
    There is also lots of key information noted below such as start time, meeting point,
    waivers that need to be completed in advance and advice on what to wear/bring with you.
    We’ll be in contact soon with final confirmation but in the meantime, if you have any
    questions, please let us know.
    Looking forward to seeing you soon!
    Kind regards,
    The Manly Sailing Team
    ###Important Information
    **Scheduling:** Please arrive in sufficient time to change and be ready for the start of
    the scheduled session time ~ 15 minutes before.
    **Waiver:** Each participant will be required to complete a waiver before participating
    in a course/experience. This can be done [online]
    (https://waiver.smartwaiver.com/w/5df05c4418b43/web) or at the office.
    **Location:** Sessions will start from Manly Sailing: Ground Floor, Manly Yacht Club,
    East Esplanade, Manly NSW 2095. Located on the wharf level underneath Manly Yacht Club.
    When in Manly, facing the Manly Ferry Wharf, turn left and follow the path around past
    East Esplanade beach until it ends. A short 10 minute walk.
    **Parking**: Parking is difficult in Manly and limited to two hours. We recommend you
    park in one of the council car parks where you will get two hours free and pay for
    additional hours thereafter or park a little further away in Little Manly where parking
    for longer periods is permitted.
    **Footwear:** You will be getting wet feet as we stop off at different beaches. Please
    wear footwear you don't mind getting wet or alternatively go barefoot. We will also
    provide you with a dry bag that you can put your shoes in and take with you on the kayak.
    **Hat:** A secure hat that can be tied on is recommended.
    **Clothing:** You may get a little wet - so a change of clothes is advisable.
    **PFD (Personal Floatation Device):** we supply PFD’s for all participants. Anyone
    declining to wear their PFD will not be allowed to participate.
    **Sun cream:** Participants should bring and use sun cream, preferably Zinc or at least
    factor 30+.
    **Dry clothes:** Participants will get wet during the session so a set of dry clothes and
    towel for afterwards is advised. An old shopping bag is recommended to carry wet gear in.
    **Refunds and changes:** Please see terms and conditions.
    **Weather:** If there is unsuitable weather for the activty we will reshedule to an
    alternative time that suits.

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    TOUR WILL START OUTSIDE BUILDING P27.
    Please either walk or catch a free shuttle bus from reception to P27 building to meet
    your guide for a 9:30am start.
    ##Internal Q Station Transport:
    Q Station offers a shuttle bus to help with transportation around the site. Since wait
    times may be lengthy, we suggest allowing some time to walk to the Visitor Centre for
    your tour. Shuttle service will be available after Ghost Tours to take you back to
    reception.
    ##What To Wear & Bring
    All tours are walking tours and include inclines, declines, steps and uneven surfaces
    through our national park heritage site. Reasonable walking mobility and fitness level is
    required as you will be standing or walking for the duration of the tour. Please wear
    enclosed flat, comfortable walking shoes. Sandals, thongs and heels are not recommended
    footwear. Tours go ahead in all weather conditions & are conducted inside and outside
    buildings. Please dress appropriately for the weather including a warm jacket and a
    raincoat. A water bottle is also recommended. There is no secure facility to store bags
    for the duration of the tour.
    ##Film & Audio Recording
    Please note that no personal film or audio recording equipment is permitted on tours.
    Still photography is permitted.
    ##Warning to Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islanders: Our tours contain stories and may
    contain images and/or visuals of deceased people.
    Find us on Instagram @qstation & @quarantineghosttours

  review signal -- a heading named this column but the model routed the content elsewhere (usually correct)
    disclaimers, meeting_point, what_to_bring, cancellation

  REWORDED -- present but not verbatim
        Additional Information: T-Shirt: A long sleeve rash vest must be worn for sun
        protection and to prevent a rash caused by the PFD. Rash vests can be purchased from

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    ##Additional Information
    **Scheduling:** In general camps are 3 days long 9.30-3pm with early morning drop off at
    8.30 onwards and late afternoon pickup until 5pm. Your order will show the start day/time
    for the first day of the camp.
    **Sign-in and out:** Please escort your child/children to the sign in desk at the back of
    Manly Sailing office (end of East Esplanade path) to complete the sign in process. Please
    let staff know if you would like your child to make their own way home and sign the
    appropriate permission slip or email us before hand.
    **Waiver:** Each participant will be required to complete a waiver before participating
    in a course / experience. This can be done [online]
    (https://waiver.smartwaiver.com/w/5d8b0b333d047/web/) or at the office.
    **Location:** Sessions will start from Manly Sailing. Our office is located on the wharf
    level underneath Many Yacht Club. When in Manly, facing the Manly Ferry Wharf, turn left
    and follow the path around past East Esplanade beach until it ends. A short 10 minute
    walk.
    **Food: Please bring recess, lunch and a water bottle** to keep them hydrated and full of
    energy to get through the day. We may stop for lunch at different beaches so please
    ensure they have their food and water ready to go in the morning.
    **Footwear:** Old trainers or wetsuit booties to provide protection from sharp objects.
    **Crocs and Thongs are not permitted** as they cannot be securely fastened to feet.
    Anyone arriving without secure footwear will not be permitted on the boats or the wharf.
    **Hat:** A secure hat that can be tied on is recommended.
    **Shorts:** Shorts over a wetsuit are essential as sailing involves sliding around on the
    boat quite a bit. If you have a wetsuit it is a good idea to bring it.
    **T-Shirt: *A long sleeve rash vest must be worn*** for sun protection and to prevent a
    rash caused by the PFD. Rash vests can be purchased from our office upon arrival.
    **Warm Top:** such as an old polar fleece, or a light wind jacket is advised to add that
    extra layer if it’s chilly.
    **PFD (Personal Floatation Device):** we supply PFD’s for all students. Students may use
    their own but we reserve the right to inspect them and if necessary insist that one of
    ours is worn. Anyone declining to wear their PFD will not be allowed to participate.
    **Sun cream:** Participants should bring their own sun cream, preferably Zinc or at least
    factor 30 or above. Sun cream should be applied at home before attending and during camps
    our staff will supervise reapplication of sun cream at the lunchtime break. If your child
    has allergies to any sunscreens please ensure they have a suitable one packed.
    **Towel:** Participants will get wet during the session so a dry towel for afterwards is
    essential.
    **Secure gear bag:** is required to store all clothes and gear. We will do our best to
    make sure students gear is secure but we do not have lock up facilities so please don’t
    bring valuables. Gear should be labelled clearly to mix-ups / lost property. We will
    store lost property for a maximum of two weeks following an event.
    **Dry clothes:** Participants will get wet during the session so a set of dry clothes for
    afterwards is advised. An old shopping bag is recommended to carry wet gear in is
    recommended.
    **Refunds and changes:** Please see terms and conditions
    **Weather:** Please see terms and conditions

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

        See You Soon: We’re looking forward to sharing a slice of farm life with you.

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Thank you for booking your Private Into the Paddock experience with us.
    Our farmers are excited to introduce you to our animals.
    Where to Meet
    Please meet us at the Wishing Well, which you’ll find out on the Farm field. See the
    photo attached so you know exactly where to head. From there, our farmers will guide you
    into the paddock for your hands-on experience.
    ![Description of image](https://cdn.filestackcontent.com/YbNIGZMRTaanuXXNaG0d)
    What to Bring
    Wear closed-in shoes
    Bring a hat and sun-safe clothing – we’ll be outdoors
    Don’t forget your water bottle
    Timing
    The experience runs for 45 minutes. We ask that you arrive 5 minutes before your start
    time so we can set off together. Once we leave the Wishing Well and head into the
    paddock, we can’t return for late arrivals (our goats don’t like turning back either!).
    Weather
    We go ahead rain, hail, or shine – unless conditions are unsafe (such as heavy rain or
    strong winds). If you haven’t heard from us with a cancellation, the experience is going
    ahead. Thankfully, we’ve got a little shelter to make things cosier when the weather
    turns. So, chuck on your gumboots and come ready for farm life!
    What to Expect
    You’ll get the chance to hand-feed our chooks, Nigerian Dwarf goats, baby lambs, and
    ducks, while learning how each of them plays a role in restoring the land. Our farmers
    will share stories and insights into life on a regenerative farm.
    See You Soon
    We’re looking forward to sharing a slice of farm life with you.
    Cheers,
    The Farm Team
    Grow • Feed • Educate
    info@thefarm.com.au (email is the best way to reach us)
    (02) 6684 7888

    ##Please check in 20 mins before your departure time.
    **Please note** that parking in Queenstown town centre is limited. You will need to allow
    additional time to find parking for your vehicle before boarding. We advise 30 minutes.
    There are parking lots available on Man Street and Church Street and free, limited
    parking down the Lake Esplanade.
    ##What to Bring
    • Non-slip shoes/ boots
    • Waterproof jacket
    • Warm sweater/fleece jacket
    • Sunscreen/sunglasses
    • Insect repellent
    • Swimwear (optional)
    • Camera
    • Overnight bag only
    • Personal medication, including for asthma and allergies
    • If booking includes an Infant, guests must provide their own car seat
    Drinks and snacks available for purchase (please note we are cashless onboard)
    **Important Information:**
    Milford Sounds natural beauty captivates all who visit, but it also comes with its share
    of natural hazards. Visit [our website](https://www.realnz.com/en/plan/natural-hazard/)
    for full information about hazards and risks in the area.
    **Please note** that our quad share cabins are mixed gender, bunk-style compartments and
    you could potentially be sharing with other guests. If you would like to change to a
    private cabin, please reach out to our contact centre on 0800 65 65 01.

        Additional Information: T-Shirt: A long sleeve rash vest must be worn for sun
        protection and to prevent a rash caused by the PFD. Rash vests can be purchased from
        our office upon arrival.

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    ##Additional Information
    **Scheduling:** In general camps are 3 days long 9.30-3pm with early morning drop off at
    8.30 onwards and late afternoon pickup until 5pm. Your order will show the start day/time
    for the first day of the camp.
    **Sign-in and out:** Please escort your child/children to the sign in desk at the back of
    Manly Sailing office (end of East Esplanade path) to complete the sign in process. Please
    let staff know if you would like your child to make their own way home and sign the
    appropriate permission slip or email us before hand.
    **Waiver:** Each participant will be required to complete a waiver before participating
    in a course / experience. This can be done [online]
    (https://waiver.smartwaiver.com/w/5d8b0b333d047/web/) or at the office.
    **Location:** Sessions will start from Manly Sailing. Our office is located on the wharf
    level underneath Many Yacht Club. When in Manly, facing the Manly Ferry Wharf, turn left
    and follow the path around past East Esplanade beach until it ends. A short 10 minute
    walk.
    **Food: Please bring recess, lunch and a water bottle** to keep them hydrated and full of
    energy to get through the day. We may stop for lunch at different beaches so please
    ensure they have their food and water ready to go in the morning.
    **Footwear:** Old trainers or wetsuit booties to provide protection from sharp objects.
    **Crocs and Thongs are not permitted** as they cannot be securely fastened to feet.
    Anyone arriving without secure footwear will not be permitted on the boats or the wharf.
    **Hat:** A secure hat that can be tied on is recommended.
    **Shorts:** Shorts over a wetsuit are essential as sailing involves sliding around on the
    boat quite a bit. If you have a wetsuit it is a good idea to bring it.
    **T-Shirt: *A long sleeve rash vest must be worn*** for sun protection and to prevent a
    rash caused by the PFD. Rash vests can be purchased from our office upon arrival.
    **Warm Top:** such as an old polar fleece, or a light wind jacket is advised to add that
    extra layer if it’s chilly.
    **PFD (Personal Floatation Device):** we supply PFD’s for all students. Students may use
    their own but we reserve the right to inspect them and if necessary insist that one of
    ours is worn. Anyone declining to wear their PFD will not be allowed to participate.
    **Sun cream:** Participants should bring their own sun cream, preferably Zinc or at least
    factor 30 or above. Sun cream should be applied at home before attending and during camps
    our staff will supervise reapplication of sun cream at the lunchtime break. If your child
    has allergies to any sunscreens please ensure they have a suitable one packed.
    **Towel:** Participants will get wet during the session so a dry towel for afterwards is
    essential.
    **Secure gear bag:** is required to store all clothes and gear. We will do our best to
    make sure students gear is secure but we do not have lock up facilities so please don’t
    bring valuables. Gear should be labelled clearly to mix-ups / lost property. We will
    store lost property for a maximum of two weeks following an event.
    **Dry clothes:** Participants will get wet during the session so a set of dry clothes for
    afterwards is advised. An old shopping bag is recommended to carry wet gear in is
    recommended.
    **Refunds and changes:** Please see terms and conditions
    **Weather:** Please see terms and conditions

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

    Hi Squidlet,
    Congrats — you’re officially on your way to experiencing the underwater world. This dive
    is the perfect intro to breathing underwater and exploring Port Stephens from a whole new
    angle. We keep things small, relaxed, and fun — whether you’re nervous or excited, you’re
    in good hands.
    Please read the next steps carefully for EACH PARTICIPANT:
    STEP 1 — Check Your Email (SDI Welcome)
    We’ll create your diver profile for you.
    ➡ Keep an eye on your inbox for a welcome email from SDI/TDI
    ➡ Follow the prompts to activate your account
    STEP 2 — Complete Your Digital Waivers (Required)
    Once your account is set up, you’ll be guided to complete:
    ➡ Medical Questionnaire
    ➡ Liability Waiver
    Important:
    If you answer YES to any medical questions, you’ll need GP clearance before diving. This
    must be completed and uploaded before moving forward.
    STEP 3 — Complete Your eLearning
    Once your medical is cleared, you’ll get access to your eLearning.
    ➡ This must be completed at least 24 hours before your course start time
    Important Stuff (Read This 👇)
    • If your eLearning isn’t completed in time, cancellation or rescheduling fees may apply
    • We dive with the tides — being late can shorten or cancel your session. Please arrive
    on time.
    • All steps (waivers + eLearning) must be completed 24 hours prior to your booking
    Any questions? Just reply or call — we’re here to help.
    We’re looking forward to our underwater adventure together!
    Rob & Sam,
    Squids Dive Co Team

    Your scuba gear hire is confirmed — we’ll have everything ready for your personalised
    fitting at Squids Dive Co HQ.
    Please bring your diving certification, photo ID, and arrive on time for sizing and
    pickup.
    We can’t wait to get you geared up for an awesome dive in Port Stephens!
    See you soon,
    Squids Dive Co Team
    HIRE TERMS
    A valid Open Water certification (or higher) and photo ID are required for all scuba
    hire.
    Gear must be returned by the agreed time. Late fees may apply.
    Lost or damaged items will incur repair or replacement costs.
    Tanks must be handled safely and never transported pressurised beyond standard
    guidelines.
    A credit card hold or security deposit may be required.
    Hire does not include a dive guide, transport, food/drinks, or additional air fills.
    Gear hired is for recreational use within Port Stephens unless pre-approved.
    If you have any questions, reply anytime — we’re here to help.

    Centre.
    We're excited to welcome your family for a day of ocean-themed fun, exploration and
    discovery. Every day brings a new adventure, with rotating activities, hands-on
    challenges and engaging experiences designed to inspire curiosity about our amazing
    marine environment.
    Your booking has been confirmed and we look forward to seeing you soon.
    What's Included:
    • Entry to the Marine Discovery Centre
    • Access to all Salty Guardians daily activities
    • Rotating challenges and themed experiences
    • Ocean-inspired crafts and creative activities
    • Marine science and conservation activities
    • Daily talks and feeding presentations
    Did you know?
    Because the Salty Guardians program changes daily, many families choose to visit multiple
    times throughout the school holidays to experience all the different activities on offer.
    For the best value, consider upgrading to an Annual Family Pass for just $100. Your pass
    provides unlimited entry to the Marine Discovery Centre for 12 months, including access
    to the Salty Guardians School Holiday Program every day it's running. With new
    activities, challenges and experiences regularly added, it's a fantastic way to enjoy
    everything the Centre has to offer all year round.
    Looking for even more holiday fun? Additional ticketed experiences are available
    throughout the school holidays and can be purchased separately:
    Mini Beasts and Native Bee Hotel Workshop
    Date: 15th July
    Time: 11:00am
    Scales & Tails Reptile Encounter
    Date: 10th July
    Time: 10:00am
    These popular sessions offer children the opportunity to get even closer to nature
    through hands-on learning and unique animal encounters. Spaces are limited, so we
    recommend booking early to avoid missing out.
    If you have any questions before your visit, please don't hesitate to contact our team.
    Thank you for supporting marine education and conservation. We can't wait to welcome your
    little Salty Guardians!
    Kind regards,
    Dolphin Discovery Centre Team

  review signal -- a heading named this column but the model routed the content elsewhere (usually correct)
    departure_info

  RECOVERED CONTENT -- reached no column (heading: text)

        CANCELLATION/ REFUND NOTES: Dive! Tutukaka Team

        CANCELLATION/ REFUND NOTES: Weather: Local conditions can vary. You are welcome to
        contact us 1–2 days prior to your trip for an updated forecast.

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Kia ora,
    Your Divemaster Course is confirmed! Please arrive at 7:45 AM on the first day of your
    course.
    Please come with evidence of 40 logged dives, active first aid and CPR certifications
    (within the past 24 months), your full medical signed off by a doctor, and your eLearning
    completed on the day of your orientation!
    IMPORTANT COURSE DETAILS:
    •Your eLearning MUST be completed PRIOR to the start of your course. It will take 12-15
    hours to complete. If you selected to purchase your eLearning from us, please give us a
    couple of days to assign the code to you, you will get an email from PADI once this is
    done. If you are purchasing your eLearning directly from PADI, you can copy this link:
    https://store.padi.com/en-au/courses/divemaster/p/60550-1B2C/
    •You MUST complete a PADI medical form and have medical clearance from a doctor PRIOR to
    your course orientation. Please make sure you have this done before you arrive for your
    orientation day.
    Course Schedule:
    •The Divemaster course is tailored to your schedule and will be based on the bookings we
    have at the time. Once you have a day set for you orientation, you will coordinate with
    our Course Director and Training Team to make a schedule that works for you!
    •Please note – most of your training will be completed at the Poor Knights Islands. The
    boat ride to the Poor Knights is approximately a one-hour boat journey, with two dive
    sites scheduled. Please note there is no return to shore between dives. If you are prone
    to seasickness, we recommend taking the appropriate medication.
    CANCELLATION/ REFUND NOTES:
    •PADI eLearning is non-refundable under all circumstances, as payment is made to PADI and
    cannot be recovered.
    •If we cancel your course due to weather, we will first attempt to reschedule you. If
    rebooking is not possible, you may be eligible for a partial refund, or we can refer you
    to complete your training elsewhere.
    •Failure to meet course requirements does not qualify for a refund, including situations
    where you are unable to participate due to medical reasons.
    What to bring: Swimsuit, towel, warm/dry clothing, reusable drink bottle, lunch/snacks.
    Our boats have a dry storage area for personal belongings.
    Weather: Local conditions can vary. You are welcome to contact us 1–2 days prior to your
    trip for an updated forecast. If any changes are required due to weather, we will notify
    you as early as possible.
    If you are arriving the day before, you’re very welcome to stop by the dive shop to check
    in and say hello.
    Please feel free to get in touch if you have any questions. We look forward to diving
    with you.
    Ngā mihi,
    Dive! Tutukaka Team

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  VERDICT   : ____________________   (hand review)

        Tour Requirements: Ngā mihi nui,

  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RAW BOOKING NOTES
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    Kia Ora!
    Thank you for booking with ExperienceKart, we’re really looking forward to hosting you.
    We hope you enjoy the experience as much as we do.
    Below are a few important details to ensure everything is well prepared for your
    experience.
    We kindly ask that you review this information prior to travel and keep your voucher
    accessible during your experience.
    Should you have any specific preferences or requirements, please feel free to let us
    know.
    If you have any questions at any time, please feel free to reach out, we’re here to help.
    # Departure Information
    - Thank you for selecting your pickup location. We'll reconfirm your exact pickup time
    closer to your travel date to ensure everything runs smoothly.
    - Please be ready at your designated location at least 10 minutes before the scheduled
    time on the day of your tour.
    - If you have not confirmed any hotel pick up with us, or any changes to your pick-up
    location must be confirmed with us with at least 24 hours prior to your tour departure.
    - Please call us or contact us by email to confirm. You will find our contact details in
    this confirmation email.
    # General Information
    - This is a fully guided adventure led by experienced and trained cave guides.
    - A comprehensive safety briefing and all technical equipment are provided.
    - Wetsuits, helmets, lights, and gumboots are supplied and must be worn throughout the
    experience.
    - Please bring swimwear and socks that can get wet.
    - Tours operate in a natural environment and are subject to weather and river conditions.
    - Participants are required to follow guide instructions at all times to ensure a safe
    and enjoyable experience.
    - Caving is an adventure activity and carries an inherent element of risk.
    # What to bring
    - Comfortable clothing suitable for the conditions.
    - Essential personal items.
    - Just bring yourself, your swimwear, a towel and a pair of socks
    - Bring shower gel and shampoo if desired
    - Towels are not supplied; you must bring your own.
    - Cameras, including Go-Pros, can’t be taken on the tour.
    # Tour Requirements
    - A good to moderate level of fitness is required.
    - Participants must be 12 years of age or older (proof of age may be required).
    - A minimum weight requirement of 45kg applies.
    - No previous caving experience is necessary.
    - Participants should be comfortable in water and capable of walking, climbing, swimming,
    and navigating uneven terrain.
    - This experience includes confined spaces, darkness, and cold-water conditions.
    - Guests must disclose any medical conditions, injuries, or medications that may affect
    their ability to participate.
    - Participants are required to complete a disclosure form covering health, physical
    ability, impairments, and water preparedness.
    - Guests must not be under the influence of alcohol, recreational drugs, or impairing
    medications.
    - All participants are required to follow guide instructions and acknowledge the inherent
    risks associated with caving.
    We look forward to having you aboard!
    Ngā mihi nui,
    ExperienceKart
    Phone: 0800 EXP KART | 0800 397 527 | +64 21 026 60993 (Outside NZ)
    Office Hours: 07:00am - 07:00pm (Mon - Sun)
    Email: bookings@experiencekart.co.nz
    Website: https://www.experiencekart.co.nz/
